Optimization loop pseudo:
```
def optimize_p(p, lr, beta_start, beta_end, iterations):
    z maybe None; p normalized sum1.
    for it in range(iter):
       q = convolution(p)
       score=2*n*q.max()
       update best
       m=q.max(); w=np.exp(beta*(q-m)); w/=w.sum()
       g = term1 + p*S
       g = 2*n*(g - g.mean())
       # maybe limit gradient
       p_new = p - lr*g
       p_new = np.clip(p_new, 0, None)
       s = p_new.sum()
       if s<1e-12: p*= (1-lr*0.1)?
       p = p_new/s
       # maybe recenter z not needed
       # adaptive lr: if score increases too much, reduce; else increase slight
```

Detailed helpers:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def compute_q_and_grad_z(p, theta, B, beta, Lq, Lg, prefix maybe):
    n=len(p)
    q = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, Lq) ** 2, Lq)[:2*n-1]
    # q small negative due to numerical, clip?
    # weights
    m = q.max()
    zexp = beta*(q-m)
    w = np.exp(zexp)
    w /= w.sum()
    # term1 = conv(w, p_rev)[n-1:2n-1]
    wfft = np.fft.rfft(w, Lg)
    pref = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Lg)
    term1 = np.fft.irfft(wfft * pref, Lg)[n-1:2*n-1]
    # S sliding sums w
    prefw = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(w)))
    S = prefw[n:n+n? wait length 2n-1. For i 0..n-1, S_i = prefw[i+n]-prefw[i]. Since w indices 0..2n-2, p len n. i+n max 2n-1, pref length 2n. Good.
    S = prefw[n:2*n] - prefw[:n]
    grad_p = term1 + p*S
    grad_F = 2.0*n*(grad_p - grad_p.mean())
    # objective value: 2n*smooth? For tracking actual score use q.max; for gradient use LSE. We can return actual too.
    smooth = (1/beta)*(-m + np.log(w.sum()))? Actually with m subtract, logsum = m + log(wsum). O=2n*(m + log(wsum))/beta? If using weights normalized, smooth = (m + log(wsum)) / beta, because LSE = m + log(sum exp(beta(q-m))). wsum = sum exp(beta(q-m)). So O=2n*(m + log(wsum))/beta.
    return q, w, grad_F, O
```
But for param gradient, need grad_F wrt p under sum constraint. Since p=exp(z)/sum, and we compute z centered? We can derive grad_theta = dot(grad_F, D_centered). Is D_centered the derivative of p wrt theta? Let's verify. Let z = Bθ, p=softmax(z). If we center z before softmax (z -= mean(z)), then derivative of p wrt theta is P (I - pp^T) B^T? The gradient of F wrt z (under sum constraint) is g_z = grad_F - mean(grad_F) (we already subtract mean). Then dF/dθ = g_z^T D, where D = derivative of centered z wrtθ. If B centered columns, D_j = B_j - mean(B_j). Then gradθ = D^T g_z. Good.

But compute grad_F with q and weights as derivative wrt p of smoothed objective with p variable not enforcing sum? We subtract mean to project to simplex. For softmax path, derivative wrt z is exactly g = grad_p - mean(grad_p) because p sum constraint. So yes.

We don't actually need `grad_F` scale? g_z = 2n*(grad_p - mean). Adam uses.

Beta choice: We want weights focus. Since q ~1/n. Use beta = k*n. `smooth` value approx max. When beta large, weights nearly active. For gradient stability, start beta=20*n and increase to 200*n. Since q max changes. Use schedule per iteration. But L-BFGS finite? We'll use Adam manually.

Param optimizer:
```
def optimize_params(theta, N, B, iters, beta0, beta1, lr, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_theta = theta.copy(); best_score = inf
    p = compute_p(B, theta); p/=p.sum()
    # maybe initial momentum
    m = zeros; v=zeros; t=0
    for it in range(iters):
        beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*(it/iters) maybe; maybe piecewise hold.
        q,w,grad_p? compute grad_z (g_z)
        score = 2*n*q.max()
        if score<best_score: update
        # Adam on theta
        gradtheta = Bcenter.T @ g_z
        # clip grad
        t+=1; update m,v, theta -= lr*m/(sqrt(v)+eps)
        # recenter theta? not needed. Clip theta to [-5,5]? z amplitude. Could project.
        # if score inf? p positive finite.
    # final evaluate maybe anneal
    return best_theta,best_score
```
Potential issue: Adam step size. gradtheta magnitude? g_z ~2n*grad_p ~O(1)? D amplitude ~1, dimension d, gradθ magnitude O(√d). lr maybe 0.01-0.1. For theta in basis, z amplitude. Use adaptive: start 0.02, if score improves maintain, else reduce. We can also normalize gradtheta by its norm.

Basis construction:
```
def make_basis(N, n_poly, n_harm):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=float)/(N-1)
    B = np.empty((N, n_poly + 2*n_harm))
    for j in range(n_poly): B[:,j] = x**(j+1) maybe exclude constant.
    for m in range(n_harm):
       angle = 2π m x
       B[:, n_poly+2*m] = np.cos(angle)
       B[:, n_poly+2*m+1] = np.sin(angle)
    # center and maybe scale each column
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
       b = B[:,j]
       B[:,j] = (b - b.mean()) / b.std() if std>0 else b
    return B
```
Using x from0 to1. Periodic basis cos/sin at x=0 and1 equal; polynomial breaks.
Maybe use x=i/N instead of N-1 for discrete uniform mapping. Doesn't matter.
Add a linear independent polynomial `x - 0.5` and `x(1-x)-1/12`, etc. The polynomial terms after centering.

Adam implementation:
```
def adam_update(param, grad, m, v, t, lr, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8, max_norm=10):
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
    mh = m/(1-beta1**t); vh=v/(1-beta2**t)
    step = mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
    gn = np.linalg.norm(grad)
    if gn > max_norm: step *= max_norm/gn?
    param = param - lr*step
    return param,m,v
```

Pseudo full code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

def next_pow2(x): ...

def fft_q(p, L):
    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, L)[:len(p)*2-1]
    return q

def grad_parts(p, w, Lg):
    n=len(p)
    term1 = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w, Lg)*np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Lg), Lg)[n-1:2*n-1]
    pref = np.empty(2*n, dtype=float); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(w)
    S = pref[n:2*n]-pref[:n]
    return term1, S

def smooth_max_grad(p, beta):
   n=len(p); Lq=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   q=...
   m=q.max()
   e=np.exp(beta*(q-m)); w=e/e.sum()
   term1,S=...
   g_p=term1 + p*S
   g_z=2.0*n*(g_p - g_p.mean())
   smooth=(m + math.log(e.sum()))/beta
   score=2.0*n*m
   return q, w, g_z, smooth, score
```
But computing Lq/Lg each call overhead; precompute in object? Top-level helpers can't close, but can pass. For param, we can precompute n, Lq, Lg.

Param basis:
```
def make_basis(N, n_poly, n_harm):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
   B=np.zeros((N, n_poly+2*n_harm))
   j=0
   for k in range(1,n_poly+1): B[:,j]=x**k; j+=1
   for m in range(1,n_harm+1):
       ang=2*np.pi*m*x
       B[:,j]=np.cos(ang); j+=1
       B[:,j]=np.sin(ang); j+=1
   for col: b-=mean, std=np.std; if std>1e-8 b/=std else 0
   return B
```
Maybe polynomial `x**k` highly correlated; standard normalization okay. But high-degree polynomial may oscillate? Degree4 fine. `np.linspace(0,1,N)` includes endpoints; for periodic sin/cos at x=1 same as 0. Good.

Compute theta from z? For initial fixed profiles, we can directly set z and project to basis? Instead optimize theta. For fallback, choose explicit z and convert to s parameter? If optimizer fails, we can return explicit profile directly without basis.

Param optimizer details:
```
def optimize_param(B, theta0, iters, start_time, end_time, beta0, beta1, lr0, seed):
   theta=theta0.copy()
   best_theta=theta.copy(); best_score=inf; best_p=None
   m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=...
   t=0; lr=lr0; stale=0
   n_prev=0
   while time.monotonic() < end_time and t<iters:
       beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*min(1.0, t/ (0.6*iters))? Actually schedule linear over iters.
       z = B @ theta
       z -= z.mean()
       # clip z
       if z.max()>ZLIM or min<-ZLIM: z=np.clip(z,-ZLIM,ZLIM)
       p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
       q,w,g_z,smooth,score = ...
       if finite score and score<best_score: update
       # if score improved last, stale=0 else stale+=1
       gradtheta = B.T @ g_z
       # clip grad norm
       t+=1; Adam update
       theta = np.clip(theta, -THETA_LIM, THETA_LIM)
       if t % 100 ==0: maybe print
       # adaptive lr: if stale>100 and lr>0.001: lr*=0.9; or if improved: lr*=1.01
```
But using time: For many restarts, we can't pass end_time fixed; we allocate. Use `optimize_param` with max_iter and time_limit independent.

Now, full z optimizer and param optimizer share heavy function. We can reduce duplicate by top-level `compute_grad_z`.

Let's sketch code:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

def q_from_p(p, L):
    spec=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    q=np.fft.irfft(spec*spec,L)
    n=len(p)
    q=q[:2*n-1]
    q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0
    if q.min()<0: q[q<0]=0
    return q

def score_from_p(p, L):
    q=q_from_p(p,L)
    return 2.0*len(p)*float(q.max())

def grad_z_from_p(p,beta,Lq,Lg,mix):
    n=len(p)
    q=q_from_p(p,Lq)
    score=2*n*q.max()
    m=q.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(q-m))
    sw=float(e.sum())
    if sw<=0 or not finite: w=np.full(...)
    else:
       w=(1-mix)*(e/sw) + mix/(2*n-1)
       w/=w.sum()
    # term1
    wr=np.fft.rfft(w,Lg)
    pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lg)
    term1=np.fft.irfft(wr*pr,Lg)[n-1:2*n-1]
    pref=np.empty(2*n); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(w)
    S=pref[n:2*n]-pref[:n]
    gq=term1 + p*S
    grad_p=2*n*gq
    dot=float(np.dot(p,grad_p))
    fz=p*(grad_p - dot)
    # if nonfinite, fallback
    return q,score,fz
```
Use beta = k*n. If `k` passed.

For `optimize_params`, Q is orthonormal. p=softmax(Q@theta). Instead of computing q each iter separately and then p? Need p for exp. We can compute z then p. `q_from_p` uses p. Could compute p via `np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()`. For numerical, if z large, clip.

`softmax` with z clipped:
```
def p_from_z(z, zclip=15.0):
   z=np.asarray(z)
   if z.max()>zclip or z.min()<-zclip: z=np.clip(z,-zclip,zclip)
   z=z-z.mean()
   p=np.exp(z)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: p=np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
   else: p/=s
   return p
```

Param optimizer:
```
def optimize_params(Q, theta0, run_start, run_end, k0,k1,lr0,flat_mix,seed):
    d=theta0.size; n=Q.shape[0]
    Lq=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    theta=theta0.copy(); m=np.zeros(d); v=np.zeros(d); t=0
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_score=inf; best_p=None
    local_best=inf; last_improve=run_start
    lr=lr0
    while time.monotonic() < run_end:
       elapsed=(time-monotonic-run_start)/(run_end-run_start)
       if elapsed<0.5: k=k0
       elif elapsed<0.8: k=(k0+k1)/2
       else: k=k1
       z=Q@theta
       p=p_from_z(z)
       q,score,fz=grad_z_from_p(p,k*n,Lq,Lg,flat_mix)
       if finite score and score<best_score:
           best_score=score; best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=time
       # gradient
       gth=Q.T @ fz
       if not finite gth: gth=np.zeros(d)
       # norm clip
       gn=np.linalg.norm(gth)
       if gn>10: gth*=10/gn
       t+=1
       m=...; v=...; theta = theta - lr*mh/(sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
       # keep theta centered? not needed; clip
       theta=np.clip(theta,-5,5)
       if score<local_best: local_best=score
       elif time-last_improve>10: lr=max(0.01,lr*0.9); local_best=score
       if t%200==0: print? avoid too many.
    return best_theta,best_score,best_p
```

Let's write `compute_grad_z` carefully:
```
def compute_grad_z(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix=0.05):
    n = len(p)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, Lq)
    q = np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, Lq)[:2*n-1]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size == 0:
       return q, np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
       q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if q.min() < 0:
       q[q < 0] = 0.0
    qmax = float(q.max())
    score = 2.0*n*qmax
    if qmax <= 0 or not finite:
       return q, score, np.zeros(n)
    beta = float(k)*n
    e = np.exp(beta*(q-qmax))
    sw = float(e.sum())
    if sw <= 0 or not finite:
       w = np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
    else:
       if mix > 0:
           w = (1.0-mix)*(e/sw) + mix/(2*n-1)
       else:
           w = e/sw
       wsum = float(w.sum())
       if wsum <=0: w=...
       else: w/=wsum
    # term1
    wf = np.fft.rfft(w, Lg)
    pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Lg)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(wf*pr, Lg)
    term1 = conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    # prefix
    # numpy cumsum allocation; use np.concatenate? 
    ps = np.empty(2*n, dtype=np.float64)
    ps[0]=0.0
    if 2*n-1>0: ps[1:] = np.cumsum(w)
    S = ps[n:2*n] - ps[:n]
    gq = term1 + p*S
    grad_p = 2.0*n*gq
    dot = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    fz = p*(grad_p - dot)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(fz)):
        fz = np.nan_to_num(fz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return q, score, fz
```
Note `spec*spec` not `spec*spec.conj()` because ordinary convolution P^2, not autocorrelation. p real, spec complex; square not magnitude. Good. Could use `np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, Lq)`.

Is `q` real? Yes. Small imaginary discarded.

For p normalized sum1, q sum ~1. But due FFT square, coefficient for negative frequencies? Since P^2 has only nonnegative powers; irfft of square of rfft of zero-padded p should give linear convolution. Confirm: rfft gives one-sided DFT. `irfft(fft(p)^2)` yields circular convolution of length L of p with p because inverse DFT of product of DFTs. Since L ≥ 2n-1, no alias. Good.

Now `make_basis`:
```
def make_basis(N, n_poly=3, n_harm=6):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    d = n_poly + 2*n_harm
    B = np.empty((N,d), dtype=np.float64)
    j=0
    for p in range(1,n_poly+1):
       B[:,j]=x**p; j+=1
    for m in range(1,n_harm+1):
       ang=2*np.pi*m*x
       B[:,j]=np.cos(ang); B[:,j+1]=np.sin(ang); j+=2
    B -= B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    # optional orthonormal QR
    Q,R = np.linalg.qr(B, mode='reduced')
    # ensure d columns; QR may produce -1? fine
    return Q
```
If B rank deficient (unlikely), Q still. d < N. We can limit d to min(d,N-1). But N large.

Potential: `x**3` with QR; okay.

Project explicit z to Q: `theta = Q.T @ z` since Q orthonormal. If Q from QR, Q.T@Q=I. Good.

`softmax z`:
```
def prob_from_z(z, zclip=15.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0: return np.array([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=zclip, neginf=-zclip)
    if z.max()>zclip or z.min()<-zclip:
       z=np.clip(z, -zclip, zclip)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <=0 or not finite:
       p = np.full(len(z), 1.0/len(z))
    else:
       p = p/s
    return p
```

`adam_update` top-level:
```
def adam_step(param, grad, m, v, t, lr, eps=1e-8, max_coord=0.25):
    m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
    v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad*grad
    mb = m/(1-0.9**t)
    vb = v/(1-0.999**t)
    step = mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+eps)
    step = np.clip(step, -max_coord, max_coord)
    new = param - lr*step
    return new, m, v
```
But t increments. For t=1, bias denom 0.1,0.001. okay.
Could normalize gradient before moment to avoid huge: `gn=np.linalg.norm(grad); if gn>1: grad=grad/gn*np.min(1,gn)`? For param, maybe clip coordinate of grad to 10. For full z, grad values small, coordinate clip of step enough.

For param, grad scale maybe 0.01, step ~lr. max_coord 0.2 okay.
For full z, step ~lr 0.1, max 0.2. Use max_coord 0.25.

Potential issue: Adam with full z and gradient values varying by p; coordinates with tiny p have tiny gradient, so v small, step may be larger? But gradient ~p*O(1); for small p, grad even smaller; step still lr. Fine.

def make_explicit_p(N, code):
    x=np.arange(N, dtype=float)/N
    z=zeros
    c=code
    if c==0: z=0
    elif c==1: r=0.6; phi=np.pi/4; z=r*(np.cos(phi)*np.cos(2πx)+np.sin(phi)*np.sin(2πx))
    elif c==2: r=0.6; phi=np.pi/3; ...
    elif c==3: z=0.6*np.sin(2πx)+0.25*np.sin(4πx)
    elif c==4: z=0.6*np.sin(2πx)-0.25*np.sin(4πx)
    elif c==5: z=0.8*(x-0.5)
    elif c==6: z=1.0*(x-0.5)**2
    elif c==7: z=0.8*np.cos(2πx)+0.3*np.cos(4πx)
    ...
    z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-4,4)
    p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
    return p
```

```
def optimize_p_projected(p0, Lq, Lg, run_start, run_end, k0,k1,lr0,mix,seed):
   n=len(p0); p=p0.copy(); p/=p.sum()
   best...
   mom=np.zeros(n); lr=lr0; t=0
   while time<end:
      q,score,gq=compute_gq(...)
      update
      g=2.0*n*(gq - gq.mean())
      # clip grad norm
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn>1e6: g*=1e6/gn
      # simple momentum: mom=0.9*mom+0.1*g; p = p - lr*mom
      # projection: clip and renorm
      p_new = p - lr*mom
      p_new = np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None)
      s=p_new.sum()
      if s<1e-12: p_new=p
      else: p_new/=s
      p = (1-floor)*p_new + floor/n  # floor maybe 1e-6? But floor changes sum? Do floor before normalize: p = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-8/n); p/=p.sum()
```

Let's detail `adam_update`: It returns new param, m, v. Need coordinate clip. For param, max_coord maybe 0.25; for z, 0.2. We can pass `max_coord` and `scale_grad`? Simpler:
```
def adam_update(param, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=0.25):
    t = max(1, t)
    m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
    v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad*grad
    mb = m/(1-0.9**t)
    vb = v/(1-0.999**t)
    step = mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8)
    step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
    return param - lr*step, m, v
```
Bias: if t is iteration number after increment? We can call with t after increment. Suppose t=1: 1-0.9=0.1. Good.
If grad nonfinite, replace zeros before update.

For param, lr maybe 0.1, max_step0.2 -> max z change0.02? Actually theta step =lr*step, step clipped0.2 => max0.02 per iter, maybe slow. We want 0.1 maybe. Set max_step=1.0 for param, lr=0.1 -> theta change up to0.1. For full z, step magnitude from Adam normalized ~1, max_step=1.0, lr=0.08 -> z change0.08. Good. Let's set param max_step=1.0, z max_step=1.0. Since Adam step after division by sqrt(v) ~O(1). Good.

`score_p_withL`:
```
def score_p_withL(p, L):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf
    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, L)[:2*n-1]
    if q.size == 0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)): q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if q.min() < 0: q[q<0]=0.0
    return 2.0*n*float(q.max())
```
`score_p` computes L.

`compute_gq` returns gq length n. It recomputes q, score. It uses `mix = min(1.0, max(0.0, mix))`.
If qmax=0, score=0, gq zeros.
When building w, `e` length 2n-1. `sw` maybe huge? exp <=1, sum <=2n-1. Good.
`prf` for prefix: Instead of `np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))` alloc two. Use empty as above. For n large, okay.
`S = total[n:2*n] - total[:n]`. For n=1, w length1, S=total[1:2]-total[0:1]=0? derivative? n=1 trivial. Fine.
`term1` slice length n. If Lg not enough? Next pow2 enough.

`compute_grad_z`: if gq nonfinite, nan_to_num. `grad_p = 2*n*gq`. dot finite? If not, fz zero.

`optimize_params` detailed:
```
def optimize_params(Q, theta0, Lq, Lg, run_start, run_end, k0, k1, lr0, mix, seed):
    n = Q.shape[0]
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    d=theta.size
    m=np.zeros(d); v=np.zeros(d); t=0
    best_score=np.inf; best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=None
    if run_end <= run_start:
       p=prob_from_z(Q@theta); score=score_p_withL(p,Lq); return theta,score,p
    dur=max(1e-6, run_end-run_start)
    local_best=np.inf; last_improve=run_start; lr=lr0
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= run_end: break
       elapsed = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now-run_start)/dur))
       if elapsed < 0.5: k=k0
       elif elapsed < 0.8: k=0.5*(k0+k1)
       else: k=k1
       z = Q @ theta
       p = prob_from_z(z, 18.0)
       q, score, fz = compute_grad_z(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix)
       if np.isfinite(score):
          if score < best_score:
             best_score=score; best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); local_best=score; last_improve=now
          if score < local_best - 1e-12:
             lr = min(0.25, lr*1.01)
       else:
          score = np.inf
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(fz)): fz = np.zeros(d)
       gth = Q.T @ fz
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(gth)): gth=np.zeros(d)
       # clip gradient coordinate maybe
       gth = np.clip(gth, -100.0, 100.0)
       t += 1
       theta, m, v = adam_update(theta, gth, m, v, t, lr, 1.0)
       theta = np.clip(theta, -4.0, 4.0)
       if now - last_improve > 20.0 and t > 50:
           lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.8); last_improve=now
    return best_theta, best_score, best_p
```
`local_best` updated only on best? Better update local_best to current score if finite, not just global, for lr adapt. Use:

if score < local_best: local_best=score; last_improve=now
```
But beta changes may increase score without actual local worsening; okay.

def optimize_z(z0,Lq,Lg,run_start,run_end,k0,k1,lr0,mix,seed):
   z=np.asarray(z0).copy(); n=z.size; z-=mean; clip 15
   m,v zeros(n)
   best... p_best
   while:
      p=prob_from_z(z,15)
      q,score,fz=compute_grad_z(p,k,Lq,Lg,mix)
      update
      g=fz
      clip g? Maybe coordinate step via Adam.
      t+=1; z,m,v=adam_update(z,g,m,v,t,lr,0.8)
      z = z - z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-15,15)
      adapt lr
   return best_z,best_score,p_best
```

`optimize_p_projected`:

def optimize_p_projected(p0,Lq,Lg,run_start,run_end,k0,k1,lr0,mix,seed):
   p=np.asarray(p0).copy(); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s>0 p/=s else uniform
   m=np.zeros(n); t=0; best...
   while:
      q,score,gq=compute_gq(p,k,Lq,Lg,mix)
      update
      g=2*n*(gq - np.mean(gq))
      if nonfinite g zeros
      g=np.clip(g,-100,100)
      t+=1
      m=0.9*m+0.1*g
      p_new = p - lr*m
      p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0)
      f = 1e-10 / max(1,n)
      p_new = np.maximum(p_new, f)
      s=p_new.sum(); if s>0 p_new/=s else p_new=p
      ln? no
      p = p_new
      adapt lr if score improves/stall. If score inf? maybe reduce lr.
   return p,best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(full_convolution(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The program optimizes a logarithmic density inside a low-dimensional
trigonometric/polynomial basis using an LSE-smoothed max-convolution
objective, then refines the full high-dimensional vector with Adam.
An optional final resampling to a larger length is used if time remains.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def make_basis(N, n_poly=3, n_harm=6):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, N))
    d = n_poly + 2 * n_harm
    B = np.empty((N, d), dtype=np.float64)
    j = 0
    for p in range(1, n_poly + 1):
        B[:, j] = x ** p
        j += 1
    for m in range(1, n_harm + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * x
        B[:, j] = np.cos(ang)
        B[:, j + 1] = np.sin(ang)
        j += 2
    B -= B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(B, mode="reduced")
    return Q


def prob_from_z(z, zclip=15.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=float(zclip), neginf=-float(zclip))
    zmax = float(z.max())
    zmin = float(z.min())
    if zmax > zclip or zmin < -zclip:
        z = np.clip(z, -zclip, zclip)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def score_p_withL(p, L):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if L < 2 * n - 1:
        L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if q.min() < 0.0:
        q[q < 0.0] = 0.0
    return 2.0 * n * float(q.max())


def score_p(p):
    return score_p_withL(p, next_pow2(2 * p.size - 1))


def compute_gq(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix=0.05):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if Lq < 2 * n - 1:
        Lq = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    if Lg < 3 * n - 2:
        Lg = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, Lq)
    q = np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, Lq)[:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size == 0:
        return q, np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if q.min() < 0.0:
        q[q < 0.0] = 0.0

    qmax = float(q.max())
    score = 2.0 * n * qmax
    if qmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(score):
        return q, score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(k) * float(n)
    e = np.exp(beta * (q - qmax))
    sw = float(e.sum())

    mix = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, mix)))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        if mix > 0.0:
            w = (1.0 - mix) * (e / sw) + mix / (2 * n - 1)
        else:
            w = e / sw
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            w = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / wsum

    wf = np.fft.rfft(w, Lg)
    prf = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Lg)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(wf * prf, Lg)
    term1 = np.asarray(conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1], dtype=np.float64)

    pref = np.empty(2 * n, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    if 2 * n - 1 > 0:
        pref[1:] = np.cumsum(w)
    S = pref[n:2 * n] - pref[:n]

    gq = term1 + p * S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gq)):
        gq = np.nan_to_num(gq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return q, score, gq


def compute_grad_z(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix=0.05):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    q, score, gq = compute_gq(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix)
    if gq.size != n:
        gq = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad_p = 2.0 * float(n) * gq
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
        grad_p = np.nan_to_num(grad_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    fz = p * (grad_p - dot)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(fz)):
        fz = np.nan_to_num(fz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return q, score, fz


def adam_update(param, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    t = max(1, int(t))
    m = 0.90 * m + 0.10 * grad
    v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * grad * grad
    mb = m / (1.0 - 0.90 ** t)
    vb = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t)
    step = mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + 1e-8)
    step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
    return param - lr * step, m, v


def optimize_params(Q, theta0, Lq, Lg, run_start, run_end, k0, k1, lr0, mix=0.05):
    Q = np.asarray(Q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = Q.shape[0]
    d = Q.shape[1]
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if theta.size != d:
        theta = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = None

    if run_end <= run_start:
        p = prob_from_z(Q @ theta, 18.0)
        s = score_p_withL(p, Lq)
        return best_theta, s, p

    m = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = float(lr0)
    dur = max(1e-6, run_end - run_start)
    local_best = np.inf
    last_improve = run_start

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= run_end:
            break
        frac = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - run_start) / dur)))
        if frac < 0.5:
            k = float(k0)
        elif frac < 0.8:
            k = 0.5 * (float(k0) + float(k1))
        else:
            k = float(k1)

        z = Q @ theta
        p = prob_from_z(z, 18.0)
        _, score, fz = compute_grad_z(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix)

        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_theta = theta.copy()
                best_p = p.copy()
                local_best = score
                last_improve = now
            if score < local_best - 1e-12:
                lr = min(0.28, lr * 1.01)

        if fz.size != d or not np.all(np.isfinite(fz)):
            fz = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)

        gtheta = Q.T @ fz
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
            gtheta = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        gtheta = np.clip(gtheta, -100.0, 100.0)

        t += 1
        theta, m, v = adam_update(theta, gtheta, m, v, t, lr, 1.0)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -4.0, 4.0)

        if now - last_improve > 20.0 and t > 50:
            lr = max(1e-4, lr * 0.80)
            last_improve = now

    return best_theta, best_score, best_p


def optimize_z(z0, Lq, Lg, run_start, run_end, k0, k1, lr0, mix=0.05):
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z, None, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    z = np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = None

    if run_end <= run_start:
        p = prob_from_z(z, 15.0)
        s = score_p_withL(p, Lq)
        return best_z, s, p

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = float(lr0)
    dur = max(1e-6, run_end - run_start)
    local_best = np.inf
    last_improve = run_start

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= run_end:
            break
        frac = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - run_start) / dur)))
        if frac < 0.5:
            k = float(k0)
        elif frac < 0.8:
            k = 0.5 * (float(k0) + float(k1))
        else:
            k = float(k1)

        p = prob_from_z(z, 15.0)
        _, score, fz = compute_grad_z(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix)

        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_z = z.copy()
                best_p = p.copy()
                local_best = score
                last_improve = now
            if score < local_best - 1e-12:
                lr = min(0.12, lr * 1.005)

        if fz.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(fz)):
            fz = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        fz = np.clip(fz, -100.0, 100.0)

        t += 1
        z, m, v = adam_update(z, fz, m, v, t, lr, 0.8)
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        z = np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)

        if now - last_improve > 25.0 and t > 80:
            lr = max(1e-5, lr * 0.85)
            last_improve = now

    return best_z, best_score, best_p


def optimize_p_projected(p0, Lq, Lg, run_start, run_end, k0, k1, lr0, mix=0.05):
    p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.inf

    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score_p_withL(p, Lq)

    if run_end <= run_start:
        return best_p, best_score

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = float(lr0)
    dur = max(1e-6, run_end - run_start)
    local_best = np.inf
    last_improve = run_start
    floor = 1e-10 / float(n)

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= run_end:
            break
        frac = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - run_start) / dur)))
        if frac < 0.5:
            k = float(k0)
        elif frac < 0.8:
            k = 0.5 * (float(k0) + float(k1))
        else:
            k = float(k1)

        _, score, gq = compute_gq(p, k, Lq, Lg, mix)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                local_best = score
                last_improve = now
            if score < local_best - 1e-12:
                lr = min(0.08, lr * 1.01)

        g = 2.0 * float(n) * (gq - float(np.mean(gq)))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.clip(g, -100.0, 100.0)

        t += 1
        mom = 0.90 * mom + 0.10 * g
        p_new = p - lr * mom
        p_new = np.nan_to_num(p_new, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p_new = np.maximum(p_new, floor)
        s2 = float(p_new.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
            p_new = p_new / s2
        else:
            p_new = p.copy()
        p = p_new

        if now - last_improve > 25.0 and t > 40:
            lr = max(1e-6, lr * 0.80)
            last_improve = now

    return best_p, best_score


def make_explicit_p(N, code):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, N))
    z = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    two = 2.0 * np.pi * x
    four = 4.0 * np.pi * x
    six = 6.0 * np.pi * x
    eight = 8.0 * np.pi * x
    c = int(code)

    if c == 0:
        z = np.zeros_like(x)
    elif c == 1:
        a = 0.5
        phi = np.pi / 4.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 2:
        a = 0.8
        phi = np.pi / 4.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 3:
        a = 0.5
        phi = np.pi / 3.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 4:
        a = 0.5
        phi = np.pi / 2.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 5:
        a = 0.5
        phi = 0.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 6:
        a = 0.5
        phi = -np.pi / 4.0
        z = a * (np.cos(phi) * np.cos(two) + np.sin(phi) * np.sin(two))
    elif c == 7:
        z = 0.60 * np.sin(two) + 0.25 * np.sin(four)
    elif c == 8:
        z = 0.60 * np.sin(two) - 0.25 * np.sin(four)
    elif c == 9:
        z = 0.60 * np.sin(two) + 0.25 * np.sin(three := six / 2.0)
    elif c == 10:
        z = 0.60 * np.sin(two) - 0.25 * np.sin(six / 2.0)
    elif c == 11:
        z = 0.70 * np.cos(two) + 0.25 * np.cos(four)
    elif c == 12:
        z = 0.70 * np.cos(two) - 0.25 * np.cos(four)
    elif c == 13:
        z = 0.80 * (x - 0.5)
    elif c == 14:
        z = 1.20 * (x - 0.5) ** 2
    elif c == 15:
        z = 0.80 * (x - 0.5) ** 3
    elif c == 16:
        z = 0.70 * np.cos(four) + 0.20 * np.cos(eight)
    elif c == 17:
        z = 0.70 * np.sin(four) + 0.20 * np.sin(eight)
    else:
        z = 0.0

    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -3.0, 3.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    p = p / p.sum()
    return p


def theta_from_p(Q, p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(Q.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(p)
    z -= float(z.mean())
    return Q.T @ z


def random_theta(d, rng, scale):
    return rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), size=int(d))


def resample_p(p, N2):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.full(N2, 1.0 / N2, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(N2))
    p2 = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    p2 = np.nan_to_num(p2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p2 = np.full(N2, 1.0 / N2, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p2 = p2 / s
    return p2


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    except Exception:
        total = 1480.0
    deadline = start + total

    if total < 300.0:
        N = 2048
    elif total < 900.0:
        N = 4096
    else:
        N = 8192

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    Q = make_basis(N, 3, 6)
    n = Q.shape[0]
    Lq = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lg = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def_uniform = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    su = score_p_withL(def_uniform, Lq)
    if su < best_score:
        best_score = su
        best_p = def_uniform.copy()

    initial_theta_list = []
    initial_items = []

    for code in range(18):
        p = make_explicit_p(n, code)
        sc = score_p_withL(p, Lq)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
        th = theta_from_p(Q, p)
        initial_items.append((sc, th))

    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if base.ndim != 1:
            base = base.reshape(-1)
        base = np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if base.size >= 32:
            sb = float(base.sum())
            if sb > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sb):
                x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, base.size)
                x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                pb = np.interp(x_new, x_old, base)
                pb = np.maximum(pb, 0.0)
                pb /= pb.sum()
                scb = score_p_withL(pb, Lq)
                if scb < best_score:
                    best_score = scb
                    best_p = pb.copy()
                thb = theta_from_p(Q, pb)
                initial_items.append((scb, thb))
    except Exception:
        pass

    initial_items.sort(key=lambda yy: yy[0])
    for _, th in initial_items:
        initial_theta_list.append(np.asarray(th, dtype=np.float64))
    if not initial_theta_list:
        initial_theta_list.append(np.zeros(Q.shape[1], dtype=np.float64))

    print("initial best score: %.8f, N=%d" % (best_score, N), flush=True)

    param_deadline = start + 0.58 * total
    direct_deadline = start + 0.73 * total
    z_deadline = deadline - 45.0
    restart = 0
    num_initial = len(initial_theta_list)

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= param_deadline:
            break
        if best_score < 1.51:
            break
        rem_param = max(0.0, param_deadline - now)
        if rem_param < 8.0:
            break

        if restart < num_initial:
            th0 = initial_theta_list[restart].copy()
        elif restart < 2 * num_initial:
            th0 = initial_theta_list[restart % num_initial].copy()
            th0 += rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=th0.size)
        elif best_p is not None:
            th0 = theta_from_p(Q, best_p)
            th0 += rng.normal(0.0, 0.16, size=th0.size)
        else:
            th0 = random_theta(Q.shape[1], rng, 0.75)

        if restart == 0:
            slice_s = 160.0
        elif restart == 1:
            slice_s = 110.0
        elif restart == 2:
            slice_s = 80.0
        else:
            slice_s = 28.0
        slice_s = min(slice_s, rem_param)
        slice_end = now + max(6.0, slice_s)

        th_best, sc_best, p_best = optimize_params(
            Q, th0, Lq, Lg, now, slice_end, 8.0, 70.0, 0.16, 0.05
        )

        if p_best is not None:
            sc_best = score_p_withL(p_best, Lq)
            if sc_best < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best
                best_p = p_best.copy()

        restart += 1
        if restart % 3 == 0 or sc_best < best_score:
            print(
                "param restart %d best=%.8f elapsed=%.1f" %
                (restart, best_score, time.monotonic() - start),
                flush=True
            )

    print("after param search best: %.8f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if best_p is not None and best_score > 1.50 and time.monotonic() < direct_deadline - 20.0:
        p_best, sc_best = optimize_p_projected(
            best_p, Lq, Lg, time.monotonic(), direct_deadline,
            18.0, 110.0, 0.025, 0.05
        )
        if p_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best):
            sc_best = score_p_withL(p_best, Lq)
            if sc_best < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best
                best_p = p_best.copy()
        print("after projected refinement best: %.8f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < z_deadline - 20.0:
        z0 = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
        z0 -= float(z0.mean())
        _, sc_best, p_best = optimize_z(
            z0, Lq, Lg, time.monotonic(), z_deadline,
            25.0, 140.0, 0.075, 0.05
        )
        if p_best is not None:
            sc_best = score_p_withL(p_best, Lq)
            if sc_best < best_score:
                best_score = sc_best
                best_p = p_best.copy()
        print("after full-z refinement best: %.8f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if (
        best_p is not None
        and best_p.size < 16384
        and best_score < 1.535
        and time.monotonic() < deadline - 130.0
    ):
        N2 = 16384
        p2 = resample_p(best_p, N2)
        Lq2 = next_pow2(2 * N2 - 1)
        Lg2 = next_pow2(3 * N2 - 2)
        sc2 = score_p_withL(p2, Lq2)
        if sc2 < best_score + 0.025:
            z2 = np.log(np.maximum(p2, 1e-300))
            z2 -= float(z2.mean())
            _, sc2, p2 = optimize_z(
                z2, Lq2, Lg2, time.monotonic(), deadline - 25.0,
                30.0, 150.0, 0.055, 0.05
            )
            if p2 is not None:
                sc2 = score_p_withL(p2, Lq2)
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
            print("after length-upgrade refinement best: %.8f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = def_uniform.copy()

    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64)
    best_p = np.nan_to_num(best_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_p = npmaximum = np.maximum(best_p, 0.0)
    s = float(best_p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        best_p = best_p / s

    final_seq = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in best_p]
    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))
        if not np.isfinite(ev):
            final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    except Exception:
        ev = best_score

    print("returned length=%d internal=%.8f evaluated=%.8f" %
          (len(final_seq), best_score, ev), flush=True)
    return final_seq
```
